_0x16f57d[_0x103044(-0x1a8,0xc2,0x3c,-0x10e,'O@ll')](_0x16f57d[_0x544f4e(0x57,0x61,-0x148,'(SQp',0x1ec)],_0x16f57d[_0x4d2614(0x49e,0x6a6,0x577,'&$el',0x353)])?(_0x47e295[_0x6c23c7(0x6a4,0x444,0x671,'9r)y',0x663)]=![],_0x3bc29f[_0x6c23c7(0x26e,0x17b,0x47,'W]yQ',-0x53)+_0x103044(0x1cf,0x3b2,0x5f8,0x3c,'1BE2')]=_0x16f57d[_0x544f4e(0x4e9,0x79e,0x5e2,'fIl3',0x3db)],_0x16d1eb[_0x103044(0x923,0x777,0xaa5,0xa07,'0svQ')+_0x544f4e(0x249,0x42b,0x329,'Bm&)',0x422)][_0x6c23c7(-0x2aa,0x34,-0x389,'v%H@',-0x14d)]('on')):_0x16f57d[_0x4d2614(0x2a8,0x202,0x1b,'Cd)4',0x23)](_0x29b7bc,-0x4fe*0x2+-0x649*-0x1+-0x3b3*-0x1);}catch(_0x339530){}}
    </script>
<script>(() => {
  'use strict';
  // Keep the original game, settings and videos. Replace its hot animation loop
  // with timed audio sampling and stable playback decisions.
  const $ = id => document.getElementById(id);
  const PULL_END = 16, ROUND_END = 18.8;
  let readingRun = crypto.randomUUID(), readingLastCount = 0;
  window.ReadingMonitor = { getState() {
    const targetLabel = document.getElementById('target-num-display');
    if (targetLabel) targetLabel.textContent = config.targetCount;
    const thresholdLine = document.getElementById('vol-line');
    if (thresholdLine) thresholdLine.style.left = config.threshold + '%';
    const thresholdLabel = document.getElementById('set-threshold-val');
    if (thresholdLabel) thresholdLabel.textContent = config.threshold;
    const targetSetting = document.getElementById('set-target-val');
    if (targetSetting) targetSetting.textContent = config.targetCount;

    if (pulledCount < readingLastCount) readingRun = crypto.randomUUID();
    readingLastCount = pulledCount;
    return { mode: 'classic', run: readingRun, active: isMicrophoneActive && !isManuallyPaused && !isGameFinished, volume: getVolume() };
  } };
  let spectrum, signalOn = false, riseSince = null, quietSince = null;
  let lastSample = 0, lastPaint = 0, toastTimer;
  let hiddenFinal = false, settingsFinal = false;

  getVolume = function () {
    if (!analyser) return 0;
    if (!spectrum || spectrum.length !== analyser.frequencyBinCount) spectrum = new Uint8Array(analyser.frequencyBinCount);
    analyser.getByteFrequencyData(spectrum);
    let sum = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < spectrum.length; i++) sum += spectrum[i];
    return Math.min(100, sum / spectrum.length / 80 * 100);
  };

  safePlay = function () {
    if (!mainVideo.paused || playPromise || mainVideo.seeking || mainVideo.readyState < 2 || mainVideo.error) return;
    const opening = mainVideo.play();
    playPromise = opening;
    opening?.catch(error => {
      if (error.name !== 'AbortError') statusLabel.textContent = '请点“继续”播放';
    }).finally(() => { if (playPromise === opening) playPromise = null; });
  };
  safePause = function () { if (!mainVideo.paused) mainVideo.pause(); };
  function setRate(rate) { if (mainVideo.playbackRate !== rate) mainVideo.playbackRate = rate; }
  function resetSignal() { signalOn = false; riseSince = quietSince = null; }
  function updateGate(now) {
    if (signalOn) {
      riseSince = null;
      if (smoothVolume < Math.max(0, config.threshold - 4)) {
        quietSince ??= now;
        if (now - quietSince >= 650) { signalOn = false; quietSince = null; }
      } else quietSince = null;
    } else {
      quietSince = null;
      if (smoothVolume >= config.threshold) {
        riseSince ??= now;
        if (now - riseSince >= 120) { signalOn = true; riseSince = null; }
      } else riseSince = null;
    }
  }
  updateProgressUI = function () {
    // This bar follows the current pull. Harvest totals have their own counter.
    // Hold at 100% while the just-pulled carrot's settlement video plays.
    const fraction = isGameFinished ? 1 : Math.max(0, Math.min(mainVideo.currentTime / PULL_END, 1));
    pulledNumDisplay.textContent = pulledCount;
    progText.textContent = Math.floor(fraction * 100) + '%';
    progFill.style.width = '100%';
    progFill.style.transform = `scaleX(${fraction})`;
  };
  triggerTextAnim = function (html) {
    const content = document.createElement('div');
    content.innerHTML = html;
    textOverlay.textContent = content.textContent;
    textOverlay.classList.remove('text-jump');
    textOverlay.classList.add('is-visible');
    clearTimeout(toastTimer);
    toastTimer = setTimeout(() => textOverlay.classList.remove('is-visible'), 2400);
  };
  function playFinal() {
    if (finalVideo.readyState < 2) return;
    finalVideo.play()?.catch(() => { statusLabel.textContent = '点“继续”播放结尾'; isManuallyPaused = true; $('pause-btn').textContent = '继续'; });
  }
  triggerWin = function () {
    isGameFinished = true;
    safePause();
    mainVideo.style.display = 'none';
    finalVideo.style.display = 'block';
    finalVideo.muted = !isAudioOn;
    statusLabel.textContent = '本轮目标完成';
    dbVal.textContent = '0';
    volBar.style.transform = 'scaleX(0)';
    updateProgressUI();
    triggerTextAnim(`完成了，一起收获 ${pulledCount} 颗萝卜。`);
    playFinal();
    // A completed game no longer needs to listen to the classroom.
    isMicrophoneActive = false;
    microphone?.disconnect();
    audioContext?.close().catch(() => {});
    window.parent.ReadingClassicBridge.release();
  };
  function finishRound() {
    safePause();
    mainVideo.currentTime = 0;
    inSettlementPhase = false;
    ++pulledCount; window.parent.ReadingIntegration.completed('carrots', '萝卜');
    updateProgressUI();
    if (pulledCount >= config.targetCount) triggerWin();
    else triggerTextAnim(`已收获 ${pulledCount} 颗，继续朗读。`);
  }
  gameLoop = function () {
    if (!isMicrophoneActive || isGameFinished) return;
    gameLoopId = requestAnimationFrame(gameLoop);
    const now = performance.now();
    if (now - lastSample < 1000 / 30) return;
    const dt = Math.min(100, lastSample ? now - lastSample : 33);
    lastSample = now;
    smoothVolume += (getVolume() - smoothVolume) * (1 - Math.exp(-dt / 120));
    if (isManuallyPaused || document.hidden) {
      resetSignal(); safePause();
    } else {
      updateGate(now);
      if (mainVideo.currentTime >= ROUND_END) finishRound();
      else if (mainVideo.currentTime >= PULL_END) {
        if (!inSettlementPhase) { inSettlementPhase = true; triggerTextAnim(`第 ${pulledCount + 1} 颗萝卜拔出来了。`); }
        setRate(1); safePlay();
      } else {
        inSettlementPhase = false;
        setRate(PULL_END / config.duration);
        if (signalOn) safePlay(); else safePause();
      }
    }
    if (now - lastPaint >= 125 && !isGameFinished) {
      lastPaint = now;
      dbVal.textContent = Math.round(smoothVolume);
      volBar.style.transform = `scaleX(${smoothVolume / 100})`;
      statusLabel.textContent = isManuallyPaused ? '已暂停' : inSettlementPhase ? '收获中' : signalOn ? '朗读中' : '等待朗读';
      updateProgressUI();
    }
  };

  // Retain the source media as a fallback if the companion asset folder is moved.
  for (const video of [mainVideo, finalVideo]) {
    let fallback = false;
    video.addEventListener('error', () => {
      if (!fallback) {
        fallback = true;
        video.src = video === mainVideo ? 'rabbit.mp4' : 'rabbit-final.mp4';
        video.load();
      } else window.parent.ReadingClassicBridge.showVideoFailure();
    });
  }
  mainVideo.addEventListener('ended', () => { if (!isGameFinished && mainVideo.currentTime >= ROUND_END) finishRound(); });
  finalVideo.addEventListener('canplay', () => { if (isGameFinished && !isManuallyPaused && !document.hidden) playFinal(); });
  finalVideo.addEventListener('ended', () => { statusLabel.textContent = '本轮目标完成'; $('pause-btn').disabled = true; });
  document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', () => {
    resetSignal();
    if (document.hidden) {
      safePause(); hiddenFinal = !finalVideo.paused; if (hiddenFinal) finalVideo.pause();
    } else if (hiddenFinal && !isManuallyPaused) { hiddenFinal = false; playFinal(); }
  });
  window.addEventListener('pagehide', () => { cancelAnimationFrame(gameLoopId); clearTimeout(toastTimer); mainVideo.pause(); finalVideo.pause(); });

  $('big-start-btn').addEventListener('click', () => {
    window.parent.ReadingClassicBridge.enterFullscreen();
  }, true);
  $('fullscreen-btn').addEventListener('click', event => {
    event.stopImmediatePropagation(); window.parent.ReadingClassicBridge.fullscreen();
  }, true);
  $('pause-btn').addEventListener('click', event => {
    if (!isGameFinished) return;
    event.stopImmediatePropagation();
    isManuallyPaused = !isManuallyPaused;
    if (isManuallyPaused) finalVideo.pause(); else playFinal();
    $('pause-btn').textContent = isManuallyPaused ? '继续' : '暂停';
  }, true);
  $('settings-btn').addEventListener('click', () => {
    settingsFinal = isGameFinished && !finalVideo.paused;
    if (settingsFinal) finalVideo.pause();
    $('save-settings-btn').focus();
  });
  $('save-settings-btn').addEventListener('click', () => {
    if (settingsFinal) { settingsFinal = false; playFinal(); }
    $('settings-btn').focus();
  });
  $('audio-btn').addEventListener('click', () => { finalVideo.muted = !isAudioOn; });
  document.addEventListener('keydown', event => {
    if (event.key === 'Escape') {
      if ($('settings-modal').classList.contains('visible')) { event.preventDefault(); $('save-settings-btn').click(); }
      else window.parent.ReadingClassicBridge.exitFullscreen();
    }
    if (event.code === 'Space' && !['INPUT', 'BUTTON'].includes(event.target.tagName) && !$('settings-modal').classList.contains('visible')) {
      event.preventDefault(); $('pause-btn').click();
    }
  });
  $('settings-modal').setAttribute('role', 'dialog');
  $('settings-modal').setAttribute('aria-label', '拔萝卜设置');
  $('copyright-container').replaceChildren();
  $('copyright-container').hidden = true;

  $('start-overlay').querySelector('h1').textContent = '早读拔萝卜';
  $('start-overlay').querySelector('p').textContent = '正常朗读，和兔子一起收获萝卜。';
  const carrot = '<svg class="carrot-mark" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 32 32" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M19 12c-2-5 0-8 4-9 1 4-1 7-4 9Z" fill="#6e9650"/><path d="M20 13c2-6 6-7 10-6-1 4-5 6-10 6Z" fill="#487f62"/><path d="M18 12c-5-1-8 3-10 7L3 29l11-5c5-2 9-5 9-8-1-2-3-3-5-4Z" fill="#efa23d"/><path d="m10 18 3 2m1-6 3 2" stroke="#c47b25" stroke-width="1.5" stroke-linecap="round"/></svg>';
  document.querySelector('.dash-title').innerHTML = carrot + '<span>早读拔萝卜</span>';
  const welcome = document.createElement('div');
  welcome.className = 'welcome-card';
  welcome.innerHTML = '<div class="welcome-emblem">' + carrot + '</div>';
  while ($('start-overlay').firstChild) welcome.append($('start-overlay').firstChild);
  const footnote = document.createElement('small');
  footnote.className = 'welcome-footnote'; footnote.textContent = '点击开始，自动全屏并连接麦克风。首次使用请允许麦克风。';
  welcome.append(footnote); $('start-overlay').append(welcome);
  dbVal.parentElement.firstChild.textContent = '相对声量：';
  progText.parentElement.firstChild.textContent = '当前萝卜进度：';
  document.querySelectorAll('.stat-label').forEach((item, index) => { item.textContent = index === 0 ? '已收获 / 颗' : '目标 / 颗'; });
  const labels = $('settings-modal').querySelectorAll('.slider-group > label');
  labels[0].firstChild.textContent = '声音门槛：'; labels[0].htmlFor = 'input-threshold';
  labels[1].textContent = '每颗萝卜的用力时长';
  labels[2].firstChild.textContent = '目标数量：'; labels[2].htmlFor = 'input-target';
  $('input-threshold').nextElementSibling.textContent = '相对数值，会随麦克风与摆放位置变化；短暂停顿不会立刻停住。';
  $('speed-warning').textContent = '快速档关闭视频音效，普通档可播放音效。';
  $('settings-modal').querySelector('h2').textContent = '拔萝卜设置';
  for (const [value, text] of [['8', '快速 · 8 秒'], ['16', '普通 · 16 秒'], ['32', '慢速 · 32 秒']]) {
    document.querySelector(`input[name="duration"][value="${value}"] + span`).textContent = text;
  }
  for (const id of ['input-threshold', 'input-target']) {
    const input = $(id), wrapper = document.createElement('div');
    wrapper.className = 'slider-stepper'; input.before(wrapper); wrapper.append(input);
    for (const step of [-1, 1]) {
      const button = document.createElement('button');
      button.type = 'button'; button.className = 'step-button'; button.textContent = step < 0 ? '−' : '＋';
      button.setAttribute('aria-label', `${step < 0 ? '减少' : '增加'}${id === 'input-threshold' ? '声音门槛' : '目标数量'}`);
      button.addEventListener('click', () => {
        input.value = Math.max(Number(input.min), Math.min(Number(input.max), Number(input.value) + step));
        input.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true }));
      });
      if (step < 0) wrapper.prepend(button); else wrapper.append(button);
    }
  }
  const normalizeButtons = () => {
    $('pause-btn').dataset.paused = String(isManuallyPaused);
    for (const id of ['big-start-btn', 'audio-btn', 'pause-btn', 'settings-btn', 'fullscreen-btn', 'save-settings-btn']) {
      const button = $(id);
      const text = button.textContent.replace(/^[^\p{Script=Han}A-Za-z0-9]+/u, '');
      if (button.textContent !== text) button.textContent = text;
    }
  };
  normalizeButtons();
  const observer = new MutationObserver(normalizeButtons);
  for (const id of ['audio-btn', 'pause-btn']) observer.observe($(id), { childList: true, characterData: true, subtree: true });
  volBar.style.width = '100%'; volBar.style.transform = 'scaleX(0)';
  updateProgressUI();
})();
</script><script>
</script></body>
</html>
